\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
in Section
4.3
.

4.

Calculate
\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
in Section
4.4
, where we use the expression of
(\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
and
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

5.

Prove
[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
in Section
4.5
by checking that the left-hand side and the right-hand side agree.

4.1
Calculation of
[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

In this subsection, our goal is to express
[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
. The proof comprises the following steps.

1.

Calculate
[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
in Lemma
7
. This is not the final version of the expression as the next two steps continue computing two terms in the result of Lemma
7
.

2.

Calculate
(-\sqrt{-1})(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}+\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
in Lemma
8
.

3.

Calculate
(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\big((\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\big)
in Lemma
9
.

4.

Summarize Steps 1–3 and give the final version of the expression of
[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
in Lemma
10
.

Lemma 7
.

The expression of
[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l][\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-\sqrt{-1})(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}+\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\big((\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\big)\end{pmatrix}.

(48)

Proof.

Observe that

\displaystyle[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\omega}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-\iota_{\omega}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}).

Recalling that
\iota_{\omega}=-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}
, we apply this to
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
and obtain

\displaystyle\iota_{\omega}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\begin{pmatrix}[l](\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\\
-\\
\begin{pmatrix}[l](\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(49)

We note that
\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha}=2\delta_{\alpha,h}
,
\iota_{h}\xi_{\alpha}=2\delta_{\alpha,h}
, and
\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\xi_{\alpha}=0
are constant functions. Thus, the formula

\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha}=0
as the contraction on a constant function is zero. Plugging these into (
49
), we obtain

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\begin{pmatrix}[l](0)\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
(2\delta_{\alpha,h})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\\
-\\
\begin{pmatrix}[l](0)\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}=-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\begin{pmatrix}[l]2\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}.

(50)

The next step is to apply
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}
on
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
:

\displaystyle\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\omega}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\begin{pmatrix}[l]2\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}.

(51)

It is not necessary to calculate the expressions of
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

and

(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

as they may represent

[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

Next, we compute
\iota_{\omega}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
. Applying
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}
to
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}).

We then apply
\iota_{\omega}=-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}
to
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
and obtain

\displaystyle\iota_{\omega}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}-\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\begin{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}-\begin{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\\
-\\
\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(52)

Using the property that
\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha}=2\delta_{h,\alpha}
,
\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\xi_{\alpha}=0
and
(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\xi_{\alpha})=0
, we can simplify the terms in (
52
) to

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\begin{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\begin{pmatrix}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}-\begin{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}0\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
2\delta_{h,\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\\
-\\
\begin{pmatrix}0\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l](\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
2\delta_{h,\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}.

Taking
-2\delta_{h,\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=-2\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\iota_{\omega}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}(\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l](\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
2\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}.

(53)

Combining (
51
) and (
53
), we find that

\displaystyle[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\omega}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-\iota_{\omega}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

is equal to

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\Big(\begin{pmatrix}[l]2\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}-\begin{pmatrix}[l](\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
2\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}\Big),

which can be simplified to

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\begin{pmatrix}[l](0-\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}-\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
2(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}+\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\big((\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\big)\end{pmatrix}.

Observing that
(0-\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})=[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha})
and
(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}-\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})=[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}]
, we have completed the proof.

∎

Lemma 8
.

\displaystyle(-\sqrt{-1})(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}+\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

is equal to

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(54)

Proof.

The proof comprises the following steps.

1.

Calculate
\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

2.

Calculate
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

3.

Combine the results from Steps 1–2.

Step 1:
The expression of
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{k},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}\end{pmatrix}.

(55)

Recalling that
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{i}=\sum_{j,k}^{n}B^{i}_{jk}\xi_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}

and

\displaystyle\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}=\sum_{j,k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{2}D^{i}_{jk}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}=\sum_{j,k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j,k\big)\\
j,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j,k\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k},

we plug these into (
55
) and cancel out the terms containing repeated frame bases, then obtain

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\frac{1}{2}(D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}+D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}})\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\frac{1}{2}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(56)

We reorder the above wedge product of frames as follows:

•

\qquad\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

=\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

=\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\frac{1}{2}(D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}+D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}})\bigg)\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\bigg)\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\frac{1}{2}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\frac{1}{2}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(u^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(u^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\bigg)\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\frac{1}{2}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
.

We then simplify (
56
) to

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix},

(57)

which is the expression of
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

Next, we apply
\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}
to (
57
). Two cases,
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
and
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}^{C}
, must be considered. We observe that if
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
, then

•

\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\big(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\big(\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\big)

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\big(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\big(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\big)

=\begin{cases}0&\text{if }\alpha=j_{l}\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}&\text{if }\alpha\neq j_{l}\end{cases}
.

The above computation leads to

\displaystyle\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\

\frac{1}{2}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}

for the case that

–

in Section

for the calculation of the simplification of the signs of permutations. Thus, we conclude that when

\displaystyle 2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)^{k}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\

\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\

+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\ {t^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

\displaystyle\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

. We conclude that when

\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(62)

Step 2:
Looking at
\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
,

\displaystyle\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=\begin{cases}0&\alpha\notin\mathbf{J}\\
2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}&\alpha\in\mathbf{J}\end{cases}.

(63)

We just need to consider the case that
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
. Applying
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}
to the formula in (
63
) such that
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
, we find that
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is equal to

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}\end{pmatrix}

(64)

for
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
. Recalling that
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{i}=\sum_{j,k}^{n}B^{i}_{jk}\xi_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}

and

\displaystyle\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}=\sum_{j,k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{2}D^{i}_{jk}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}=\sum_{j,k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j,k\big)\\
j,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j,k\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k},

we plug these into (
64
) and cancel out the terms containing repeated frames. Thus,
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
for
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
has the following expression:

\displaystyle 2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]\frac{1}{2}(D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}+D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}})\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}+D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}+D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\\
\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

We reorder the wedge product of frames as follows:

•

\qquad\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\bigg(B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}

=\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}

=\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{k-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi_{i_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{i_{p}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}

=\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)}\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\frac{1}{2}\begin{pmatrix}[l]D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}

=\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\qquad\begin{matrix}[l]\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}\wedge\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\wedge\cdots\\
\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}}\end{matrix}

=\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}}u^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}
.

Using the above properties, we calculate
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
for
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
as a sum of eight groups of terms:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{i_{k}}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+
