Lemma 9
.

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\big((\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(75)

Proof.

Let us calculate the first half,

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}(\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}).

(76)

With the contraction computation

\displaystyle\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=\begin{cases}0&\,h\notin\mathbf{J}\\
2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
h^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{h^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{h^{{}^{\prime}}\}}&\,h\in\mathbf{J}\end{cases},

we use
j_{l}
to replace
h
and simplify (
76
) to

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}}(\iota_{\xi_{j_{l}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\bigg(2\,\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}}\bigg).

(77)

Recall that
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha}=\displaystyle\sum_{s,t}B^{\alpha}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}
. We plug this into (
77
) and cancel out the terms containing repeated wedge product of frames, obtaining

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}(\iota_{\xi_{j_{l}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi_{j_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{j_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix})\wedge\bigg(2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}}\bigg).

(78)

The contractions in the first parenthesis are computed as follows:

\displaystyle\iota_{\xi_{j_{l}}}(\xi_{j_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}})=2\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\iota_{\xi_{j_{l}}}(\xi_{j_{l}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}})=2\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}.

Thus, we simplify (
78
) to

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}2\begin{pmatrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\big(2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}}\big).

(79)

We observe that

•

\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
,

•

\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
.

After ordering the wedge product of frames, we can simplify (
79
) to

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(80)

We then calculate the sign of permutations:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=1,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Rearranging (
80
), we conclude that the simplified expression of the first half is:

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(81)

We then look at the second part,

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}).

(82)

Using the contraction computation

\displaystyle\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=\begin{cases}0&\,h\notin\mathbf{I}\\
2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
h,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{h\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{h\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}&\,h\in\mathbf{I}\end{cases},

(83)

we use
i_{k}
to replace h and simplify (
82
) to

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\bigg(2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg).

(84)

We plug
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha}=\displaystyle\sum_{s,t}B^{\alpha}_{st^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t^{{}^{\prime}}}
into (
84
) and cancel out the terms containing repeated wedge product of frames, obtaining

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k^{{}^{\prime}}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\end{pmatrix})\wedge\bigg(2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg).

(85)

The contractions in the first parenthesis are computed as follows:

\displaystyle\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}}(\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}})=-2\xi_{i_{k}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}}(\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}})=-2\xi_{s}.

Thus, we simplify (
85
) to

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]-2B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{i_{k}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}-2B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{s}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\bigg(2\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg),

(86)

in which

•

\xi_{i_{k}}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
,

•

\xi_{s}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
.

Thus, after ordering the wedge product of frames, we simplify (
86
) to

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(87)

We calculate the sign of permutations:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=1
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{k},s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We then calculate (
87
):

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}(-1)B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(88)

Finally, we assemble (
81
) and (
88
) and complete the proof of Lemma
9
:

\displaystyle(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bigg((\iota_{\xi_{k}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{k}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

∎

Lemma 10
.

\displaystyle[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l][\bar{\partial},\iota_{\omega}]\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge[\bar{\partial},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{\alpha}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{(\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{s}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
(-1)B^{t^{{}^{\prime}}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(89)

Proof.

Recall that (
48
) is

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l][\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\\
-\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-\sqrt{-1})(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}+\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\big((\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\big)\end{pmatrix}.

We prove the lemma by substituting the expressions of
(-\sqrt{-1})(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}+\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
and
(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\big((\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\big)
into the above formula.

Recall that Lemma
8
states that
(-\sqrt{-1})(\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}+\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}})(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is equal to

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

(90)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

(91)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

(92)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}.

(93)

Likewise, recall that Lemma
9
states that
(-\frac{\sqrt{-1}}{2})\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{n}\big((\iota_{\xi_{h}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})-(\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{h^{{}^{\prime}}}}\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\iota_{\xi_{h}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\big)
is equal to

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}

(94)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}

(95)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}

(96)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}.

(97)

We combine the similar terms in the above sums:

•

(
96
) + (
90
) is equal to

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\bigg(B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}-B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}.

(98)

Consider the constraint

\displaystyle B^{\alpha}_{i_{k}i^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}-B^{i_{k}}_{i_{k}\alpha}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}.

Applying it to the terms in (
98
), we obtain the following simplified expression:

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}.

(99)

•

We can add the case that
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}=\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}
to the summation inside (
92
) since
D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}=0
. Then,
(
92
) + (
94
) is equal to

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}+B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}\end{pmatrix}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}.

(100)

Recall the constraint

\displaystyle B^{j_{l}}_{j_{l}\alpha}-B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\big)\\
\alpha,j_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\big)}.

Applying it to the terms in (
100
), we obtain the following simplified expression:

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}B^{j_{l}}_{j_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}.

(101)

•

(
91
) + (
97
) is equal to

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\big(B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}-B^{\alpha}_{si^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}\big)\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}.

(102)

Recall the constraint

\displaystyle B^{i_{k}}_{s\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}}-B^{\alpha}_{si_{k}}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{s}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)}.

Applying it to the terms in (
102
), we obtain the following simplified expression:

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{s}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}.

(103)

To match the right-hand side, which we discuss later, we split the sum in (
103
) into two parts:

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{\alpha}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

(104)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{s}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix},

(105)

where (
104
) is obtained from

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s=\alpha}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{s}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix},

where
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{\alpha\}\big)=\{\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\}
since we assumed
\alpha<i_{1}
.

•

(
93
) + (
95
) is equal to

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\bigg(\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}-B^{\alpha}_{j_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}\bigg)\\
\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}.

(106)

Recall the constraint

\displaystyle B^{\alpha}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}t^{{}^{\prime}}}-B^{t^{{}^{\prime}}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)}.

Applying it to the terms in (
106
), we obtain the following simplified expression:

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
(-1)B^{t^{{}^{\prime}}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}.

(107)

We can now summarize the expression of
[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
by summing up

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l][\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\end{pmatrix}

with (
99
), (
101
), (
104
), (
105
), and (
107
):

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l][\bar{\partial},\iota_{\omega}]\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge[\bar{\partial},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{\alpha}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{(\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{s}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
(-1)B^{t^{{}^{\prime}}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix},

which is the right-hand side in the statement of Lemma
10
.
∎

4.2
Calculation of

(\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

Lemma 11
.

\displaystyle(\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}=-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(108)

Proof.

Recall that
\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}=-(-1)^{n^{2}}\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star
. Our proof comprises the following steps:

1.

Calculate
\star(\xi_{\alpha})
.

2.

Calculate
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha})
.

3.

Calculate
\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha})
and conclude the expression of
(\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
.

Step 1:
We take
\star
on
\xi_{\alpha}
and obtain

\displaystyle\star(\xi_{\alpha})=2^{1+0-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}.

(109)

Step 2:
We apply
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}
to (
109
) and obtain

\displaystyle 2^{1-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{n^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(110)

Recalling that
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}=\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{s,t}D^{k}_{st}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t}
and
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}=\displaystyle\sum_{s,t}B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{st}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t}
, we cancel out the terms containing repeated frame bases. The formula of (
110
) is simplified to

\displaystyle 2^{1-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}\wedge\cdots\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]\frac{1}{2}(D^{k}_{k\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}+D^{k}_{\alpha k}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k})\end{pmatrix}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{n^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(111)

We use the notation

\displaystyle\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)}=\begin{cases}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\qquad\text{if }j<k\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\qquad\text{if }k<j\end{cases}

to simplify the formula

\displaystyle D^{i}_{jk}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}=D^{i}_{kj}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}=D^{i}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)},

where
D^{i}_{jk}=-D^{i}_{kj}
. Thus, the wedge product of frames in (
111
) can be simplified to

\displaystyle\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}\wedge\cdots\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]\frac{1}{2}(D^{k}_{k\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}+D^{k}_{\alpha k}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k})\end{pmatrix}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}\wedge\cdots\wedge D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k\alpha\big)}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,k-1,\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big),k+1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1,\cdots,n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}

(112)

and

\displaystyle\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{n^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,l+1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1,\cdots,n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}.

(113)

Using (
112
) and
(
113
) to replace the corresponding terms in (
111
), we obtain the expression of
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha})
:

\displaystyle 2^{1-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,k-1,\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big),k+1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1,\cdots,n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,l+1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1,\cdots,n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(114)

Step 3:
Take
\star
on (
114
). Using the formula

\displaystyle\star(\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{1,\cdots,n}\wedge\xi_{1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}})=2^{n+n-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=2^{n}(-1)^{n^{2}},

we compute
\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha})
as

\displaystyle 2^{1-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]2^{n}(-1)^{n^{2}}\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,k-1,\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big),k+1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
2^{n}(-1)^{n^{2}}\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,l+1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{n^{2}}2\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,k-1,\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big),k+1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)]\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,l+1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(115)

The signs of permutation in (
115
) can be simplified as follows:

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,k-1,\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big),k+1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,z\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,k-1,\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big),k+1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,z\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big),1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,z\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,k,1,\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

(116)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,l+1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}l^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,1,\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1).

(117)

We plug (
116
) and (
117
) into (
115
) and simplify the expression of
\star\,\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha})
to

\displaystyle 2(-1)^{n^{2}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

Thus, we arrive at the final expression:

\displaystyle(\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{-1}\big(-(-1)^{n^{2}}\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\star(\xi_{\alpha})\big)\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

∎

4.3
Calculation of

\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

Lemma 12
.

\displaystyle\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

(118)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,i^{c}_{k}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)^{p}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(119)

Proof.

Recall that
\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{\star}=-(-1)^{n^{2}}\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star
. Our proof comprises the following steps:

1.

Calculate
\star(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

2.

Calculate
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

3.

Calculate
\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
and conclude the expression of
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

Step 1:
We take
\star
on
\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
and obtain

\displaystyle\star(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=2^{p+q-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}.

(120)

Step 2:
We apply
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}
to (
120
):

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

(121)

Recall that

\displaystyle\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}=\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{s,t}D^{k}_{st}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}=\displaystyle\sum_{s,t}B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{st}\xi_{s}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{t}.

We plug these into (
121
) and cancel out the terms containing repeated frames in the wedge product, obtaining the following expression of
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\star(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
:

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{i^{c}_{k}r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{ri^{c}_{k}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{r_{1}r_{2}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r_{1}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r_{2}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(122)

We use the notation

\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)}=\begin{cases}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\qquad\text{if }j<k\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\qquad\text{if }k<j\end{cases}

to rewrite the formula

\displaystyle D^{i}_{jk}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}=D^{i}_{kj}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}=D^{i}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)},

where
D^{i}_{jk}=-D^{i}_{kj}
. Thus, the wedge product of frames in (
122
) can be simplified as follows.

•

\displaystyle\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{i^{c}_{k}r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{ri^{c}_{k}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\bigg)\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge\bigg(\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}r\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}r\big)}\bigg)\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
