B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},u^{{}^{\prime}},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-1}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(128)

We simplify the signs of permutation in Section
5.3
. The details can be found in Lemmas
25
–
28
. We simplify the expression of
\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
to

\displaystyle 2(-1)^{n^{2}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,i^{c}_{k}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)^{p}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

We can now calculate
\sqrt{-1}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\big(-(-1)^{n^{2}}\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star\big)(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
as

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,i^{c}_{k}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)^{p}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix},

which completes the proof of Lemma
12
.
∎

4.4
Calculation of
\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

Lemma 13
.

\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is equal to (a) + (b) + (c) where

(a):

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}

(129)

(b):

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},(\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)^{p+1}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}

(130)

(c):

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\\
\alpha,r\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(131)

Proof.

Recall that
\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}=-(-1)^{n^{2}}\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star
. Our calculation comprises four steps:

1.

Calculate

\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

2.

Calculate

\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

3.

Calculate

-(-1)^{n^{2}}\sqrt{-1}\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

4.

Decompose the result from Step 3 into three parts, where the first part is related to the expression of
(\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
and the second part is related to the expression of
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

Completing Step 4 gives the formulas in the statement of Lemma
13
.

Step 1:
Using the definition of the Hodge star, we obtain

\displaystyle\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=2^{p+1+q-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},\mathbf{J}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}},

(132)

where
\{i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\}=\mathbf{I}^{C}
and
\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\}={\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}
are the complement sets of
\mathbf{I}
and
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}
.

Step 2:

We apply
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}
to (
132
):

\displaystyle\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=2^{p+1+q-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},\mathbf{J}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},\mathbf{J}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(133)

Recalling that

\displaystyle\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{i}=\sum_{j,k}^{n}B^{i}_{jk}\xi_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}=\sum_{j<k}^{n}D^{i}_{jk}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k},

we plug these into (
133
) and obtain

\displaystyle 2^{p+1+q-n}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},\mathbf{J}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]\frac{1}{2}(D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{i^{c}_{k}\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\alpha i^{c}_{k}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}})\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{i^{c}_{k}r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{ri^{c}_{k}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{r\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\alpha r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{r_{1}r_{2}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r_{1}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r_{2}}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\\
\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},\mathbf{J}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c}}\wedge\begin{pmatrix}[l]B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\alpha}\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{ur}\xi_{u}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\end{pmatrix}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c}}\\
\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(134)

Recall that
D^{i}_{jk}=-D^{i}_{kj}
. Let us use the notation

\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)}=\begin{cases}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\qquad\text{if }j<k\\
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\qquad\text{if }k<j\end{cases}
and obtain the following expression:

\displaystyle D^{i}_{jk}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}=D^{i}_{kj}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}=D^{i}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(jk\big)}.

Using this trick, we can simplify the terms in (
134
) as follows:

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\frac{1}{2}(D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{i^{c}_{k}\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\alpha i^{c}_{k}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}})\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{i^{c}_{k}r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{ri^{c}_{k}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k}}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{r\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}+D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\alpha r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{r_{1}r_{2}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r_{1}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r_{2}}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}[l]D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}\alpha\big)}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}r\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}r\big)}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r\alpha\big)}\\
+\\
\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1}r_{2}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1}r_{2}\big)}\end{pmatrix}.

Next, we rearrange the terms in (
134
). The calculation for ordering the wedge product of frames is as follows. In the first parenthesis, we have:

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}\alpha\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}
,

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}r\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k}r\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}
,

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r\alpha\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big)}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}
,

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k-1}}\wedge\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1}r_{2}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1}r_{2}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{k+1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\widehat{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i^{c}_{n-p}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}
.

In the second parenthesis, we have:

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c}}\wedge B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\alpha}\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}
,

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c}}\wedge B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}
,

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c}}\wedge B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},u^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
,

•

\quad\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c}}\wedge B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{ur}\xi_{u}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{r}\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\xi_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}}\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},u^{{}^{\prime}},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)}\wedge\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}
.

We conclude that
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is equal to

\displaystyle 2^{p+1+q-n}\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},\mathbf{J}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\end{pmatrix}

(135)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2^{p+1+q-n}\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

(136)

\displaystyle+

Step 3:

Recall that

\displaystyle\star(\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}})=2^{(|\mathbf{I}^{C}|+|{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}|-n)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}.

Let us take
\star
on
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
. Each term in
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is as follows:

•

applying
\star
to (

), we calculate

\displaystyle\star(\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}})=2^{n-p-q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}};

•

applying
\star
to (
136
), we calculate

\displaystyle\star(\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}})=2^{n-p-q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}},

where
\xi_{\alpha}
is at the head of the wedge product of frames since we use the assumption that
\alpha<i_{1}
;

applying

to (

\displaystyle 2^{n-p-q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{n-p-q}\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big),\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix},

where
\xi_{\alpha}
is at the head of the wedge product of frames since we use the assumption that
\alpha<i_{1}
.

Recall that

\displaystyle\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=-\sqrt{-1}(-1)^{n^{2}}\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}).

As we have already applied
\star
to each term in
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
, we just need to multiply by
-\sqrt{-1}(-1)^{n^{2}}
to obtain the expression of
\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
:

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]-2\sqrt{-1}(-1)^{n^{2}}\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},\mathbf{J}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}

i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

Step 4:

Cancel out
(-1)^{n^{2}}
and rearrange the expression according to the expressions of

and
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
. The expression of
\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is a sum of three parts.

+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(159)

This part is related to the expression of

.

r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\

+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
