157
), which is equal to

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\\
\alpha,r\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(161)

∎

4.5
Proof of

[\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

Proof.

Let us look at the right-hand side first. Recall that
\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is a sum of three parts:
part I
is (
159
),
part II
is (
160
), and
part III
is (
161
).

The expression of
part I
is

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

As a result of (
108
) in the statement of Lemma
11
, the expression of
\sqrt{-1}(\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}
is

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq\alpha}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=1}^{n}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(162)

Thus, we can split (
162
) into a sum of two parts:

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}

(163)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\in I}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

The term (
163
) is just the expression of
(\sqrt{-1}\partial^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
. Therefore, we can conclude that
part I
is equal to

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\sqrt{-1}(\partial^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\in I}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\sqrt{-1}(\partial^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\in I}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
k,\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix},

(164)

where we use
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,k\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=-\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
k,\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

The expression of
part II
is

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},(\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)^{p+1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(165)

Recall (
119
) in Lemma
12
, which shows that
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{-1}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,i^{c}_{k}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{r_{2}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\frac{1}{2}D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)^{p}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\big)\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}r}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(166)

Comparing (
166
) with (
165
), the two formulas are almost the same except for a minus sign difference and an extra term in the first summation of (
166
) such that
i^{c}_{k}=\alpha
. We can conclude that
part II
is equal to

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\alpha\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{\alpha}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}.

(167)

Now, let us sum up
part I
,
part II
, and
part III
:

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\sqrt{-1}(\partial^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\in I}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
k,\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\alpha\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{\alpha}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\\
\alpha,r\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(r,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{pmatrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{pmatrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

Rearranging the previous sum, we obtain the expression of the right-hand side:

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l](\sqrt{-1}\partial^{*}\xi_{\alpha})\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)\\
k,\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{k}_{\mathrm{order}\big(k,\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{l^{{}^{\prime}}}_{l^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\alpha\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{\alpha}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i^{c}_{k}\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\displaystyle\sum_{r\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\\
\alpha,r\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{i^{c}_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\displaystyle\sum_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l](-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)B^{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

We complete the proof by observing that the expression of the right-hand side is completely equal to the left-hand side (
89
):

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l][\bar{\partial},\iota_{\omega}]\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}-\xi_{\alpha}\wedge[\bar{\partial},\iota_{\omega}](\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})\\
+\\
(-2\sqrt{-1})\begin{pmatrix}[l]\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)D^{i_{k}}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\begin{matrix}[l]B^{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)\\
i_{k},\alpha\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{\alpha}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{(\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i_{k}\in\mathbf{I}}\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,i_{k}\big)\\
\alpha,i_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
D^{s}_{\mathrm{order}\big(i_{k},\alpha\big)}\xi_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big)}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\end{matrix}\\
+\\
\displaystyle\sum_{j_{l}\in\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\displaystyle\sum_{t^{{}^{\prime}}\in{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
(-1)B^{t^{{}^{\prime}}}_{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\alpha}\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)}\end{matrix}\end{pmatrix}\end{pmatrix}.

∎

5
Appendix II

5.1
Calculation of the coefficients in

\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{i}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j})

In this section, we simplify the following three formulas:

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,k-1,k,i,k+1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix},

(168)

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},l^{{}^{\prime}},i,(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix},

(169)

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}},i,(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix},

(170)

which are from (
18
) in Lemma
2
.

Lemma 14
.

\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,k-1,k,i,k+1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=(-1)^{n^{2}}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=-\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,k-1,k,i,k+1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}k,i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,k-1,k+1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=-\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,k,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,k-1,k+1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n\\
k,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,\widehat{k},\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,k,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,k-1,k+1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Expression (
168
) is simplified to

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(171)

We continue the computation with the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Expression (
171
) is then simplified to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}.

∎

Lemma 15
.

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},l^{{}^{\prime}},i,(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=(-1)^{n^{2}}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},l^{{}^{\prime}},i,(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}l^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify expression (
169
) to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(172)

We continue the computation with the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We obtain the simplification of (
172
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}.

∎

Lemma 16
.

\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}},i,(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=-(-1)^{n^{2}}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i,j^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\begin{matrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,(l-1)^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}},i,(l+1)^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)&=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
&=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{matrix}
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i}\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

Expression (
170
) is then simplified to

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(173)

We continue the computation with the following properties:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
l^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{l^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}},i,1,\cdots,\widehat{i},\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,\widehat{j^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We then simplify (
173
) as follows:

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=-(-1)^{n^{2}}.

∎

5.2
Calculation of the coefficients in
\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

In this section we calculate the following formulas:

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},u^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},u^{{}^{\prime}},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big),\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

which are from (
143
) to (
150
) in Section
4.4
. The purpose is to simplify the expression of
\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

Lemma 17
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
17
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(174)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{p+q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (
174
) to

\displaystyle(-1)^{p+q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(175)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{(p+q)(n-p+n-q)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}
.

We simplify (
175
) to

\displaystyle(-1)^{(p+q)(p+q+1)}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},\alpha\big)\\
\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
17
.
∎

Lemma 18
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=-(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
18
to

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}.

(176)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{p-1+q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=-(-1)^{p+q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (
176
) to

\displaystyle-(-1)^{p+q}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}.

(177)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=(-1)^{(p+q)(n-p+n-q)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}
.

We simplify (
177
) to

\displaystyle-(-1)^{p+q}(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
18
.
∎

Lemma 19
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=(-1)^{n^{2}}\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big)\\
\alpha,r\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha,r\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

to

\displaystyle-(-1)^{n^{2}}(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\

\displaystyle=

Lemma 20
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha,i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\

\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\

,

•

\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k},\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\alpha,\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

to

Lemma 21
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=-(-1)^{n^{2}}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-q}}^{c}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
21
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(184)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{p+q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=(-1)^{(p+q)(n-p+n-q)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}
.

We simplify (
184
) to

\displaystyle(-1)^{p+q}(-1)^{(p+q)(n-p+n-q)}(-1)^{n^{2}}=(-1)^{(p+q)(p+q+1)}(-1)^{n^{2}}=-(-1)^{n^{2}},

which completes the proof of Lemma
21
.
∎

Lemma 22
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

Lemma 23
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

to

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}
.

We simplify (
188
) to

\displaystyle(-1)(-1)^{(p+q)(p+q+1)}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma

.
∎

Lemma 24
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\\

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

.

to

) to

\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\

\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{\alpha\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}
.

We simplify (

) to

\displaystyle(-1)^{p+1}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma

.

5.3
Calculation of the coefficients in
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

In this section, we calculate the following signs:

•

\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
