i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-1}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

•

\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},u^{{}^{\prime}},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-1}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}
,

which are from (

) in Section

. We need these signs to simplify the expression of
\star\,\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\star(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
and compute
\xi_{\alpha}\wedge\sqrt{-1}\partial_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
.

Lemma 25
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
25
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(192)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\

\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{p+q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (
192
) to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)^{p+q}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\

(193)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{(n-p+n-q)(p+q)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

.

We simplify (

) to

\displaystyle(-1)^{(p+q)}(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(i^{c}_{k},r\big)\\
r,i^{c}_{k}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma

Lemma 26
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}[l]-(-1)^{n^{2}}\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{1},\cdots,i^{c}_{k-1},\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k+1},\cdots,i^{c}_{n-p}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i^{c}_{k},\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
26
to

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}.

(194)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

,

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

i^{c}_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}^{C}\backslash\{i^{c}_{k}\})\cup\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\

.

We simplify (

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(195)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\

\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

.

We simplify (
195
) to

\displaystyle(-1)^{p+q+1}(-1)^{(n-p+n-q)(p-2+q)}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1)^{p+q}(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1)^{(p+q)(p+q+1)}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(r_{1},r_{2}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\})\cup\{i^{c}_{k}\}\big)\\
i^{c}_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r_{1},r_{2}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma

.

Lemma 27
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-1}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=-(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n-p+l-1}
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-1}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n-p+l}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
27
to

\displaystyle(-1)^{n-p+l-1}(-1)^{n-p+l}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(196)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{(n-p+n-q)(p-q+1)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{(n-p+n-q)(p-q+1)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}
.

We simplify (
196
) to

\displaystyle(-1)(-1)^{n^{2}}(-1)^{(n-p+n-q)(p-q+1)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)(-1)^{n^{2}}(-1)^{(p+q)(p+q+1)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\}\\
\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
27
.
∎

Lemma 28
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1}}^{c},u^{{}^{\prime}},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1}}^{c},\cdots,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{n-1}}^{c}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big),\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{\ {j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{I}^{C}\cup\{r\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(({\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\})\cup\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma

to

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{(n-p+n-q)(p-1+q-1)}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{n^{2}}
.

We simplify (
198
) to

\displaystyle(-1)^{q}(-1)^{(n-p+n-q)(p-1+q-1)}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{q}(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}(-1)^{n^{2}}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}u^{{}^{\prime}},r,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{r\},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c}\}\big)\\
{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}}^{c},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\backslash\{u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Since
(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}=(-1)^{p+q}
, we can simplify
(-1)^{q}(-1)^{p+q}=(-1)^{p+2q}=(-1)^{p}
, which completes the proof of Lemma
28
.

Lemma 29
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}(-1)^{k}\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{k}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
29
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)^{k}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
29
.

.

Lemma 33
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=(-1).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{k-1}
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{k}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=1
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
33
to

\displaystyle(-1)^{k-1}(-1)^{k}=(-1),

which completes the proof of Lemma
33
.

Lemma 34
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
34
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(202)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{k},s,\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (

) to

\displaystyle(-1)(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma

.

Lemma 35
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\cup\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=1
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
35
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
35
.
∎

Lemma 36
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
36
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(203)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (
203
) to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
36
.

5.5

\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\circ\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
such that
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}

In this section, we calculate

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

in Lemmas
37
–
44
, which are needed in (
61
) to simplify the expression of
\bar{\partial}_{\mathfrak{g}}\circ\iota_{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\alpha}}(\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
such that
\alpha\in\mathbf{J}
.

Lemma 37
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=(-1).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{k-1}
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{k}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=1
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
37
to

\displaystyle(-1)^{k-1}(-1)^{k}=-1,

which completes the proof of Lemma
37
.
∎

Lemma 38
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}[l]-(-1)^{k}\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}.

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k},t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)^{k}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
38
to

\displaystyle-(-1)^{k}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha,\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

(204)

which completes the proof of Lemma
38
.

Lemma 39
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{k},s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
39
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
39
.

Lemma 40
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=-\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}i_{1},\cdots,i_{k-1},s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k+1},\cdots,i_{p},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k},\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
40
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(205)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},i_{k},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (
205
) to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}s,t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
i_{k},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{I}\backslash\{i_{k}\})\cup\{s\}\big),\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\alpha^{{}^{\prime}})\cup{t^{{}^{\prime}}}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma

.

Lemma 41
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
41
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(206)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\quad\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=1
.

We simplify (
206
) to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
41
.

Lemma 42
.

to

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (
207
) to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

(208)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

(209)

which completes the proof of Lemma
42
.

Lemma 43
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},t^{{}^{\prime}}\big)\\
t^{{}^{\prime}},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

,

\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma

to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

,

j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}t^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\

.

We simplify (

) to
