Lemma 44
.

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}[l]\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\end{pmatrix}=-\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

Proof.

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}j^{{}^{\prime}}_{1},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l-1},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l+1},\cdots,\widehat{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}},\cdots,j^{{}^{\prime}}_{q}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify the left-hand side of the equation in Lemma
44
to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big).

(211)

We use the following properties of the sign of permutation:

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
,

•

\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l}\}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\\
=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)
.

We simplify (
211
) to

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathrm{order}\big(t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\big),\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},\mathrm{order}\big((\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\backslash\{j^{{}^{\prime}}_{l},\alpha^{{}^{\prime}}\})\cup\{t^{{}^{\prime}},u^{{}^{\prime}}\}\big)\end{smallmatrix}\Big),

which completes the proof of Lemma
44
.

6
Appendix III

The proof of proposition
1
is as follows:

6.1
The proof of 1.

Proof.

The expression on the left-hand side is

\displaystyle\star(f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}f\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{\prime},\cdots,n^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\bar{f}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{\prime},\cdots,n^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}.

The expression on the right-hand side is

\displaystyle\star(\overline{f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}})=

\displaystyle\star(\bar{f}\,\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\star(\bar{f}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\bar{f}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{\prime},\cdots,n^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\bar{f}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{\prime},\cdots,n^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C}\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\bar{f}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{\prime},\cdots,n^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}.

∎

6.2
The proof of 2.

Proof.

Because of

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(f\,\zeta_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\zeta_{i_{k}})=(\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}f)\wedge\zeta_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge\zeta_{i_{k}}+\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}(-1)^{j}f\,\zeta_{i_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge(\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\zeta_{i_{j}})\wedge\cdots\wedge\zeta_{i_{k}},

it is sufficient to complete the proof on the neighborhood chart through two parts. The first part is to show
\overline{\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,f}=\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\,\overline{f}
; the second part is to show
\overline{\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi}=\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\overline{\xi^{{}^{\prime}}}=\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}
. We achieve this using the local frame
\{v_{i},v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
spanning
\mathfrak{g}^{\mathbb{C}}|_{U}
and the corresponding dual frame
\{\xi_{i},\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
spanning
\mathfrak{g}^{*\mathbb{C}}|_{U}
.

Step 1:
Let us look at the first part. Suppose
f=g+\sqrt{-1}h
where
u,v
are real-valued functions. We take the Lie algebroid differential

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}f=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\rho(v_{i})f\big)\xi_{i}+\big(\rho(v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i})f\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\rho(v_{i})g+\sqrt{-1}\rho(v_{i})h\big)\xi_{i}+\big(\rho(v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i})g+\sqrt{-1}\rho(v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i})h\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}.

Setting
\alpha_{i}:=v_{i}+v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\in\Gamma(U,\mathfrak{g}|_{U})
and
\beta_{i}:=\sqrt{-1}(v_{i}-v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i})\in\Gamma(U,\mathfrak{g}|_{U})
, we can rewrite
v_{i}=\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{i}-\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})
and
v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}=\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{i}+\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})
and plug these expressions into the expression of
\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}f
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}\big(\rho(\alpha_{i}-\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})g+\sqrt{-1}\rho(\alpha_{i}-\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})h\big)\xi_{i}\\
+\\
\big(\rho(\alpha_{i}+\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})g+\sqrt{-1}\rho(\alpha_{i}+\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})h\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bigg(\big(\rho(\alpha_{i})g+\rho(\beta_{i})h\big)-\sqrt{-1}\big(\rho(\beta_{i})g-\rho(\alpha_{i})h\big)\bigg)\xi_{i}\\
+\\
\bigg(\big(\rho(\alpha_{i})g-\rho(\beta_{i})h\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}+\sqrt{-1}\big(\rho(\beta_{i})g+\rho(\alpha_{i})h\big)\bigg)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\end{pmatrix}.

Taking the conjugation, we obtain the expression of
\overline{\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}f}
:

\displaystyle\overline{\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}f}=\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bigg(\big(\rho(\alpha_{i})g+\rho(\beta_{i})h\big)+\sqrt{-1}\big(\rho(\beta_{i})g-\rho(\alpha_{i})h\big)\bigg)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\\
+\\
\bigg(\big(\rho(\alpha_{i})g-\rho(\beta_{i})h\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}-\sqrt{-1}\big(\rho(\beta_{i})g+\rho(\alpha_{i})h\big)\bigg)\xi_{i}\end{pmatrix},

(212)

where the action of
\rho(\alpha_{i})
and
\rho(\beta_{i})
on
g
and
h
are real-valued functions.

On the other side, the Lie algebroid differential on the conjugation of
f
is

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\bar{f}=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\rho(v_{i})\bar{f}\big)\xi_{i}+\big(\rho(v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i})\bar{f}\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\rho(v_{i})g-\sqrt{-1}\rho(v_{i})h\big)\xi_{i}+\big(\rho(v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i})g-\sqrt{-1}\rho(v^{{}^{\prime}}_{i})h\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}\big(\rho(\alpha_{i}-\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})g-\sqrt{-1}\rho(\alpha_{i}-\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})h\big)\xi_{i}\\
+\\
\big(\rho(\alpha_{i}+\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})g-\sqrt{-1}\rho(\alpha_{i}+\sqrt{-1}\beta_{i})h\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bigg(\big(\rho(\alpha_{i})g-\rho(\beta_{i})h\big)-\sqrt{-1}\big(\rho(\beta_{i})g+\rho(\alpha_{i})h\big)\bigg)\xi_{i}\\
+\\
\bigg(\big(\rho(\alpha_{i})g+\rho(\beta_{i})h\big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}+\sqrt{-1}\big(\rho(\beta_{i})g-\rho(\alpha_{i})h\big)\bigg)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}\end{pmatrix}.

(213)

Comparing (
212
) to (
213
), we prove the equality
\overline{\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}f}=\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\bar{f}
.

Step 2:
Recalling (
3
) and (
4
), which are the Lie algebroid differentials on the frames
\xi_{i}
and
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{i}
, we calculate

\displaystyle\overline{\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{i}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j<k}^{n}\overline{A^{i}_{jk}\xi_{j}\wedge\xi_{k}}+\sum_{j,k}^{n}\overline{B^{i}_{jk}\xi_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j<k}^{n}\overline{A^{i}_{jk}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}+\sum_{j,k}^{n}\overline{B^{i}_{jk}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi_{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j<k}^{n}\overline{A^{i}_{jk}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}+\sum_{j,k}^{n}(-\overline{B^{i}_{jk}})\xi_{k}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j<k}^{n}\overline{A^{i}_{jk}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}+\sum_{j,k}^{n}(-\overline{B^{i}_{kj}})\xi_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k}^{n}C^{i}_{jk}\xi_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}+\sum_{j<k}^{n}D^{i}_{jk}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{j}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{k}.

According to (
1
) and (
2
), the coefficients
A,B,C,
and
D
are constrained:

\displaystyle\overline{A^{k}_{ij}}=D^{k}_{ij}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\overline{B^{k}_{ij}}=-C^{k}_{ji}.

Therefore, we can conclude that
\overline{\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi_{i}}=\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\xi^{{}^{\prime}}
.

∎

6.3
The proof of 3.

Proof.

It is enough to verify the identity on a local chart
U
. The local expression of the Hodge star on
f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\in\mathcal{A}^{p,q}_{\mathfrak{g}}(U)
is

\displaystyle\star(f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})=2^{p+q-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)f\,\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}.

Applying the Hodge star to
\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\star(\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}})

\displaystyle=\star\bigg(\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)2^{n-q+n-p-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,\\
{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}\wedge\xi_{\mathbf{I}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)2^{n-q+n-p-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,\\
{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\\
{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)2^{n-q+n-p-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,\\
{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I}^{C},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=2^{n-q+n-p-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}.

(214)

Observe that
\star^{2}(f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}})
is equal to

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)f\,\star(\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{I}^{C}}\wedge\xi_{{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}}).

(215)

We plug (
214
) into (
215
) and obtain

\displaystyle 2^{p+q-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)2^{n-q+n-p-n}\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,\\
\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C},\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\mathbf{I},\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}},\mathbf{I}^{C},{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{C}\end{smallmatrix}\Big)f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}\Big(\begin{smallmatrix}1,\cdots,n,1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}}\\
1^{{}^{\prime}},\cdots,n^{{}^{\prime}},1,\cdots,n,\end{smallmatrix}\Big)(-1)^{(p+q)(n-p+n-q)}f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{n^{2}}(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}f\,\xi_{\mathbf{I}}\wedge\xi^{{}^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{J}^{{}^{\prime}}},

where
(-1)^{(p+q)^{2}}=(-1)^{p+q}
.

∎

6.4
The proof of 4.

See reference
S. Evens 1999
.

6.5
The proof of 5.

Proof.

Suppose
\psi\in\mathcal{A}^{p,q}_{\mathfrak{g}}\subset\mathcal{A}^{p+q}_{\mathfrak{g}}(M)
and
\phi\in\mathcal{A}^{p+q-1}_{\mathfrak{g}}(M)
, then we can use Stoke’s theorem on
\phi\wedge\overline{\star\psi}
, which is a
(2n-1)
-Lie algebroid form, and obtain

\displaystyle\int_{M}\langle\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\phi\wedge\overline{\star\psi}),\Omega\rangle=0.

(216)

Plugging the equality
\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\phi\wedge\overline{\star\psi})=(\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\phi)\wedge\overline{\star\psi}+(-1)^{p+q-1}\phi\wedge\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\overline{\star\psi})
into (
216
), we obtain

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\int_{M}\langle(\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\phi)\wedge\overline{\star\psi},\Omega\rangle+(-1)^{p+q-1}\int_{M}\langle\phi\wedge\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\overline{\star\psi}),\Omega\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\phi,\psi\big)_{\mathcal{A}^{p+q}_{\mathfrak{g}}(M)}+(-1)^{p+q-1}\int_{M}\langle\phi\wedge\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\overline{\star\psi}),\Omega\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\phi,\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\psi\big)_{\mathcal{A}^{p+q}_{\mathfrak{g}}(M)}+(-1)^{p+q-1}\int_{M}\langle\phi\wedge\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\overline{\star\psi}),\Omega\rangle.

We plug
(-1)^{n^{2}}(-1)^{p+q}\star\star=\mathrm{id}
in front of the second
\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}
and obtain

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\big(\phi,\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\psi\big)_{\mathcal{A}^{p+q}_{\mathfrak{g}}(M)}+(-1)^{p+q-1}\int_{M}\langle\phi\wedge(-1)^{n^{2}}(-1)^{p+q}\star\star\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(\overline{\star\psi}),\Omega\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\phi,\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\psi\big)_{\mathcal{A}^{p+q}_{\mathfrak{g}}(M)}+(-1)^{p+q-1}\int_{M}\langle\phi\wedge(-1)^{n^{2}}(-1)^{p+q}\star\overline{\star\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\star\psi},\Omega\rangle\text{potential problem}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\phi,\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\psi\big)_{\mathcal{A}^{p+q}_{\mathfrak{g}}(M)}+(-1)(-1)^{n^{2}}\big(\phi,\star\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\star\psi\big),

which implies
\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}=(-1)^{n^{2}}\star\mathrm{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}\star
.

∎
